SELECT * FROM (SELECT department, sum(salary) AS "Salaries for department"  FROM employees  GROUP BY department HAVING sum(salary) > 50000 )t1  
inner join (SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600)t2 on 1=1 
left join (SELECT sales_person, MAX(commission) AS "highest" FROM sales GROUP BY sales_person )t3 on 1=1;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION bst01(id integer)
    RETURNS INTEGER
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
  AS
  $$
  declare
    count integer;
  begin
    count = (select
               count(*)
             FROM  t_user a
             where a.user_id=id);
    return count;
  end;
$$

create or replace function  getRcord2() RETURNS  text  AS $$
    declare
       rec record;
       v_text text;
       c_tmp cursor  for select * from tmp;
    begin
        v_text:='';
       open c_tmp;
       loop
           fetch  c_tmp into rec;
           exit when not FOUND;
           v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;
       end loop;
       close c_tmp;
    return v_text;
    end;
    $$ language plpgsql;
db_type: Greenplum
[SQL 42] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json

    "databases": [],

    "columns": []
}

    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["tmp"],
    "columns": ["*"]
}

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.f_loop_insert(i_k integer[], i_v text[])
    RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    STRICT
   AS $function$
   declare 
     i_length int := array_length(i_k,1); 
     i int;
     s timestamp;
     e timestamp;
   begin 
     s := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'start: %', s;
     for i in 1..i_length loop
       insert into test values (i_k[i], i_v[i]); 
     end loop;
     e := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'end: %, %', e, e-s; 
   end;
   $function$;
db_type: Greenplum
[SQL 43] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```

{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": ["public"],
    "tables": ["test"],
    "columns": []
}

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION gettable(rows integer)
    RETURNS TABLE(col1 INTEGER, col2 TEXT)
    AS $$
    BEGIN
      RETURN QUERY SELECT i * 2, i || '_text'
        FROM generate_series(1, rows, 1) as t(i);
    END;
    $$ LANGUAGE PLPGSQL;
db_type: Greenplum

    "columns": ["col1", "col2", "i"]
}

create or replace function test_case(score integer)
    returns varchar as
$ty$
declare
    ty varchar;
begin
    case
        when score between 0 and 60 then ty = (select name from tmp limit 1);
        when score between 60 and 80 then ty = (select user_name from t_user limit 1);
        when score between 80 and 100 then ty = ((select class_name from t_class limit 1));
        else ty = 'no select';
        end case;
    return ty;
end;
$ty$ language plpgsql;
db_type: Greenplum
[SQL 45] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json

    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}

{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["tmp", "t_user", "t_class"],
    "columns": ["name", "user_name", "class_name"]
}

CREATE TABLE uk_price_paid
(
? ? price UInt32,
? ? date Date,
? ? postcode1 LowCardinality(String),
? ? postcode2 LowCardinality(String),
? ? type Enum8('terraced' = 1, 'semi-detached' = 2, 'detached' = 3, 'flat' = 4, 'other' = 0),
? ? is_new UInt8,
? ? duration Enum8('freehold' = 1, 'leasehold' = 2, 'unknown' = 0),
? ? addr1 String,
? ? addr2 String,
? ? street LowCardinality(String),
? ? locality LowCardinality(String),
? ? town LowCardinality(String),
? ? district LowCardinality(String),
? ? county LowCardinality(String)
)
ENGINE = MergeTree
ORDER BY (postcode1, postcode2, addr1, addr2);

SELECT
    town,
    district,
    count() AS c,
    round(avg(price)) AS price,
    bar(price, 0, 5000000, 100)
FROM uk_price_paid
WHERE date >= '2020-01-01'
GROUP BY
    town,
    district
HAVING c >= 100
ORDER BY price DESC
LIMIT 100

CREATE TABLE lineorder_flat
ENGINE = MergeTree ORDER BY (LO_ORDERDATE, LO_ORDERKEY)
AS SELECT
    l.LO_ORDERKEY AS LO_ORDERKEY,
    l.LO_LINENUMBER AS LO_LINENUMBER,
    c.C_NAME AS C_NAME,
    s.S_PHONE AS S_PHONE,
    p.P_NAME AS P_NAME,
    p.P_MFGR AS P_MFGR
FROM lineorder AS l
INNER JOIN customer AS c ON c.C_CUSTKEY = l.LO_CUSTKEY
INNER JOIN supplier AS s ON s.S_SUPPKEY = l.LO_SUPPKEY
INNER JOIN part AS p ON p.P_PARTKEY = l.LO_PARTKEY;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 64] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {"lineorder_flat"}, Columns: {"LO_ORDERDATE", "LO_ORDERKEY"}

}

SELECT
    sourceIP,
    sum(adRevenue) AS totalRevenue,
    avg(pageRank) AS pageRank
FROM rankings_1node ALL INNER JOIN
(
    SELECT
        sourceIP,
        destinationURL AS pageURL,
        adRevenue
    FROM uservisits_1node
    WHERE (visitDate > '1980-01-01') AND (visitDate < '1980-04-01')
) USING pageURL
GROUP BY sourceIP
ORDER BY totalRevenue DESC
LIMIT 1

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW default.zipkin_spans
ENGINE = URL('http://127.0.0.1:9411/api/v2/spans', 'JSONEachRow')
SETTINGS output_format_json_named_tuples_as_objects = 1,
    output_format_json_array_of_rows = 1 AS
SELECT
    lower(hex(trace_id)) AS traceId,
    case when parent_span_id = 0 then '' else lower(hex(parent_span_id)) end AS parentId,
    lower(hex(span_id)) AS id,
    operation_name AS name,
    start_time_us AS timestamp,
    finish_time_us - start_time_us AS duration,
    cast(tuple('clickhouse'), 'Tuple(serviceName text)') AS localEndpoint,
    cast(tuple(
        attribute.values[indexOf(attribute.names, 'db.statement')]),
        'Tuple(""db.statement"" text)') AS tags
FROM system.opentelemetry_span_log
db_type: MySQL

SELECT
    groupArray(value) OVER w1 AS frame_values_1,
    first_value(value) OVER w1 AS first_value_1,
    last_value(value) OVER w1 AS last_value_1,
    groupArray(value) OVER w2 AS frame_values_2,
    first_value(value) OVER w2 AS first_value_2,
    last_value(value) OVER w2 AS last_value_2
FROM wf_frame
WINDOW
    w1 AS (PARTITION BY part_key ORDER BY order ASC),
    w2 AS (PARTITION BY part_key ORDER BY order ASC Rows BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW)
ORDER BY
    part_key ASC,
    value ASC;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 123] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {"w2", "wf_frame", "w1", "part_key"}, Columns: {"first_value_2", "first_value", "frame_values_1", "groupArray", "value", "OVER", "first_value_1", "last_value", "frame_values_2", "last_value_2", "last_value_1"}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["w2", "wf_frame", "w1", "part_key"],
    "columns": ["first_value_2", "first_value", "frame_values_1", "groupArray", "value", "OVER", "first_value_1", "last_value", "frame_values_2", "last_value_2", "last_value_1"]
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["wf_frame"],
    "columns": ["value", "part_key", "order"]
}
```

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION bst01(id integer)
    RETURNS INTEGER
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
  AS
  $$
  declare
    count integer;
  begin
    count = (select
               count(*)
             FROM  t_user a
             where a.user_id=id);
    return count;
  end;
$$

    "columns": []
}

create or replace function  getRcord2() RETURNS  text  AS $$
    declare
       rec record;
       v_text text;
       c_tmp cursor  for select * from tmp;
    begin
        v_text:='';
       open c_tmp;
       loop
           fetch  c_tmp into rec;
           exit when not FOUND;
           v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;
       end loop;
       close c_tmp;
    return v_text;
    end;
    $$ language plpgsql;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 219] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json

    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["tmp"],
    "columns": ["*"]
}

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.f_loop_insert(i_k integer[], i_v text[])
    RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    STRICT
   AS $function$
   declare 
     i_length int := array_length(i_k,1); 
     i int;
     s timestamp;
     e timestamp;
   begin 
     s := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'start: %', s;
     for i in 1..i_length loop
       insert into test values (i_k[i], i_v[i]); 
     end loop;
     e := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'end: %, %', e, e-s; 
   end;
   $function$;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 220] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": ["public"],
    "tables": ["test"],
    "columns": []
}
```
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION gettable(rows integer)
    RETURNS TABLE(col1 INTEGER, col2 TEXT)
    AS $$
    BEGIN
      RETURN QUERY SELECT i * 2, i || '_text'
        FROM generate_series(1, rows, 1) as t(i);
    END;
    $$ LANGUAGE PLPGSQL;

    "columns": ["col1", "col2"]
}

create or replace function test_case(score integer)
    returns varchar as
$ty$
declare
    ty varchar;
begin
    case
        when score between 0 and 60 then ty = (select name from tmp limit 1);
        when score between 60 and 80 then ty = (select user_name from t_user limit 1);
        when score between 80 and 100 then ty = ((select class_name from t_class limit 1));
        else ty = 'no select';
        end case;
    return ty;
end;
$ty$ language plpgsql;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 222] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["tmp", "t_user", "t_class"],
    "columns": ["name", "user_name", "class_name"]
}

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION bst01(id integer)
    RETURNS INTEGER
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
  AS
  $$
  declare
    count integer;
  begin
    count = (select
               count(*)
             FROM  t_user a
             where a.user_id=id);
    return count;
  end;
$$
db_type: MySQL

    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}

{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["t_user"],
    "columns": ["user_id"]
}

create or replace function  getRcord2() RETURNS  text  AS $$
    declare
       rec record;
       v_text text;
       c_tmp cursor  for select * from tmp;
    begin
        v_text:='';
       open c_tmp;
       loop
           fetch  c_tmp into rec;
           exit when not FOUND;
           v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;
       end loop;
       close c_tmp;
    return v_text;
    end;
    $$ language plpgsql;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 470] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["tmp"],
    "columns": ["*"]
}
```
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.f_loop_insert(i_k integer[], i_v text[])
    RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    STRICT
   AS $function$
   declare 
     i_length int := array_length(i_k,1); 
     i int;
     s timestamp;
     e timestamp;
   begin 
     s := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'start: %', s;
     for i in 1..i_length loop
       insert into test values (i_k[i], i_v[i]); 
     end loop;
     e := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'end: %, %', e, e-s; 
   end;
   $function$;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 471] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": ["public"],
    "tables": ["test"],
    "columns": []
}
```
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION gettable(rows integer)
    RETURNS TABLE(col1 INTEGER, col2 TEXT)
    AS $$
    BEGIN
      RETURN QUERY SELECT i * 2, i || '_text'
        FROM generate_series(1, rows, 1) as t(i);
    END;
    $$ LANGUAGE PLPGSQL;
db_type: MySQL

    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": ["col1", "col2", "i"]
}

create or replace function test_case(score integer)
    returns varchar as
$ty$
declare
    ty varchar;
begin
    case
        when score between 0 and 60 then ty = (select name from tmp limit 1);
        when score between 60 and 80 then ty = (select user_name from t_user limit 1);
        when score between 80 and 100 then ty = ((select class_name from t_class limit 1));
        else ty = 'no select';
        end case;
    return ty;
end;
$ty$ language plpgsql;
db_type: MySQL
[SQL 473] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {}, Columns: {}
result``json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": [],
    "columns": []
}
```
expect_result: ```json
{
    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
    "tables": ["tmp", "t_user", "t_class"],
    "columns": ["name", "user_name", "class_name"]
}

create or replace FUNCTION test_sql_scheam.ger_ref(refcursor) RETURNS refcursor AS
$$
BEGIN
 OPEN $1 FOR SELECT name FROM test_sql_scheam.tmp;
 RETURN $1;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

FETCH ALL IN funccursor;
COMMIT;

SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)

[SQL 522] DB: {}, Schema: {}, Tables: {"pg_attribute", "pg_class", "pg_namespace", "pg_attrdef", "vals"}, Columns: {"adbin", "oid", "nextval", "adrelid", "nspname", "typnotnull", "pg_get_expr", "attnum", "pg_catalog", "attnotnull", "attname", "relname", "typtype"}

    "databases": [],
    "schemas": [],
